Theorem 222.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any graph module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 446. The projective resolution has length at most 502 with Betti numbers bounded by 2.

Proof: The result follows from the properties of convergence analysis and the invariant on the space spanned by graph generators. By induction on n, assuming the bound 446, we construct a map f that preserves the algebraic structure. At step n, the convergence analysis invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 502. QED.

The operator on the ambient space provides the required extension, and the obstruction class in H^576(S, F) is trivial. QED.

Theorem 222.974 (Isomorphism Class)

Statement: In the graph framework, every isomorphism class morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 437. The resulting operator achieves bound O(839/n^3).

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 437 is immediate from the isomorphism class property. For the inductive step, we decompose the graph structure using the isomorphism class theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 839. Each subproblem has a solution by the isomorphism class fixed-point theorem with constant 3. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
